[BR-008] There is no deleted state in this module (`INV-BE-066`).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `active` | `API-NOTES-232390` | title and body non-empty; `pinned` boolean if supplied | row inserted; `pinned` defaults to `false`; `ASM-BE-357` |\n| `active` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | note exists and `status = 'active'` | `status` set to `archived`; all other data intact [BR-007; US-002-04] |\n| `archived` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | note exists and `status = 'archived'` | `status` set to `active`; title, body, pinned and tags unchanged [BR-008; US-002-05] |\n| `active` | `active` | `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` | note exists; input valid | supplied fields or tag links change; `status` unchanged |\n| `archived` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` | note exists; input valid | same as above (`ASM-BE-362`, `Q-BE-351`) |\n\n**Illegal transitions.**\n\n| Attempt | Result | Status |\n|---|---|---|\n| archive an `archived` note | refused, conflict, no change [US-002-04] | status `GAP-BE-309`; error code `NOTE_ALREADY_ARCHIVED` (proposed in `backend.error-model`) |\n| unarchive an `active` note | refused, conflict, no change (`ASM-BE-363`, `Q-BE-352`) | status `GAP-BE-309`; error code `NOTE_NOT_ARCHIVED` (`GAP-BE-565`) |\n| any operation on a note that does not exist | refused, no change | 404 `NOT_FOUND` [US-002-02, US-002-05, US-002-06]; for archive and tag operations `ASM-BE-369` |\n\n**Reads.** `API-NOTES-045877` reads in either state and changes none. [US-002-06]\n\n**Concurrency.** Each state change is one conditional `UPDATE ... WHERE id = $1 AND status = $2`. If it matches no row, a follow-up read distinguishes not found from conflict. Two concurrent PATCH requests are applied last-write-wins on the fields each one supplies (`ASM-BE-365`, `Q-BE-353`). There is no broadcast and no merge logic. [US-005-03; BR-015]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nStatuses of error responses follow `backend.error-model`. Where an input does not state a status, the item names the gap.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-001 Create a note\n**Given** an authenticated caller and a payload with a non-empty `title` and `body`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** a note is stored with a server-assigned unique id, `status` `active`, `pinned` `false`, and `created_at` and `updated_at` set, and 201 is returned with the created note.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-01; BR-005; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-002 Create a pinned note\n**Given** a valid payload with `pinned` `true`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** 201 is returned and the stored note has `pinned` `true`.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-02; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-pinned` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-003 Reject an invalid create\n**Given** a payload with a missing, null, empty or whitespace-only `title` or `body`, or a `pinned` that is not a boolean,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` is returned and no note is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-03; BR-005; `ASM-BE-359`; `ASM-BE-371`. Length limits are `GAP-BE-035`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-missing-title`, `test-notes-create-missing-body`, `test-notes-create-whitespace`, `test-notes-create-pinned-not-boolean` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-004 Get a note\n**Given** an existing note id, active or archived,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned with the full note: id, title, body, tags, archive status, the `pinned` flag, and timestamps.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06; AC-002-06 criteria in stories.md.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-active`, `test-notes-get-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-005 Get a note that does not exist\n**Given** an id for no note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOT_FOUND` is returned with no partial data in the body.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-006 Edit a note\n**Given** an existing note and valid values for any of `title`, `body`, `pinned`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned with the updated note, `updated_at` is advanced, and `created_at` is unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; BR-006; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-fields`, `test-notes-patch-updated-at` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Status.** PUT versus PATCH is `GAP-BE-309`; only PATCH is defined. Tag edits through PATCH are `GAP-BE-566`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-007 Partial update preserves other fields\n**Given** an existing note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called with a payload containing only `pinned`,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `pinned` is updated, and `title`, `body` and tags are unchanged. A field absent from the payload is never written.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-02; BR-006; `INV-BE-008`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-pinned-only`, `test-notes-patch-omitted-fields-untouched` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-008 Edit a note that does not exist\n**Given** an id for no note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOT_FOUND` is returned and no data is modified.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-009 Reject an invalid edit\n**Given** a PATCH payload that is empty, contains a field other than `title`, `body`, `pinned` (including `id`, `status`, `created_at`, `updated_at`), sets `title` or `body` empty, or sets `pinned` to a non-boolean,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` is returned and nothing is written.\n- **Traces to.** BR-005; BR-006; NFR-012; `ASM-BE-360`, `ASM-BE-361`, `ASM-BE-371`. Not directly stated in an input for the empty payload and unknown-field cases.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-empty-body`, `test-notes-patch-unknown-field`, `test-notes-patch-blank-title`, `test-notes-patch-status-field-rejected` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-010 Add a tag\n**Given** an existing note and a valid tag name not on the note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned and the note's tag list includes the tag. The tag row is created if it does not exist.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03; first acceptance criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n- **Status.** Tag name format, length and case rules are `GAP-BE-035`; comparison is exact per `ASM-BE-370`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-011 Adding an existing tag creates no duplicate\n**Given** a tag already on the note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called with the same tag name,\n**Then** 200 is returned, no second link is created, and `updated_at` is not advanced (`ASM-BE-366`).\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03; second acceptance criterion; `INV-BE-064`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-duplicate`, `test-notes-tag-add-concurrent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-012 Remove a tag\n**Given** an existing note carrying the tag,\n**When** `API-NOTES-150096` is called,\n**Then** the link is removed, the tag no longer appears on the note, and the note's other tags and data are unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03 (story text: \"add or remove tags\"); `INV-BE-065`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-remove` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-150096`.\n- **Status.** The success status and response body are `GAP-BE-567`. A tag not on the note returns 404 `NOT_FOUND` (`ASM-BE-364`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-013 Archive a note\n**Given** an existing note with `status` `active`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `status` is `archived`, and title, body, `pinned` and tags are intact. No data is deleted.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04; first acceptance criterion; BR-007; `INV-BE-066`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-014 Archive an already-archived note\n**Given** a note with `status` `archived`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** a conflict response with error code `NOTE_ALREADY_ARCHIVED` is returned and nothing changes.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04; second acceptance criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-twice` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n- **Status.** The status code is `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-015 Unarchive a note\n**Given** an existing note with `status` `archived`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `status` is `active`, and all original data is preserved.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05; first acceptance criterion; BR-008; `INV-BE-065`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive`, `test-notes-archive-unarchive-roundtrip` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-016 Unarchive a note that does not exist\n**Given** an id for no note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOT_FOUND` is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05; second acceptance criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-017 Unarchive a note that is already active\n**Given** a note with `status` `active`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** a conflict response with error code `NOTE_NOT_ARCHIVED` is returned and nothing changes.\n- **Traces to.** Not stated in any input. Derived by symmetry with US-002-04 (`ASM-BE-363`, `Q-BE-352`).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-active` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n- **Status.** The status code is `GAP-BE-309`; the error code is `GAP-BE-565`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-018 Missing note on archive and tag operations\n**Given** an id for no note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-359413` or `API-NOTES-150096` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOT_FOUND` is returned and nothing is created or changed.\n- **Traces to.** Derived from the 404 policy in `backend.error-model`; no input states it for these operations (`ASM-BE-369`).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-not-found`, `test-notes-tag-add-note-not-found`, `test-notes-tag-remove-note-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-019 Authentication required\n**Given** a request with no valid credential,\n**When** any of the seven operations is called,\n**Then** 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED` is returned and nothing is read or changed.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (401 documented for authenticated endpoints); `backend.auth-model`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-requires-auth` (planned).\n- **API.** all seven.\n- **Status.** The credential form is `Q-BE-012`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-020 Input is safe against injection\n**Given** note title, body or tag values containing SQL or script metacharacters,\n**When** any write operation is called,\n**Then** every value is bound as a parameter, never concatenated into a statement, and the value is stored and returned verbatim.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-injection-title`, `test-notes-injection-tag`, `test-notes-injection-path-param` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`, `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-021 A rejected request changes nothing\n**Given** any request that returns a 4xx,\n**When** the response is sent,\n**Then** the stored notes, tags and links are exactly as before the request.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01 (no note persisted); US-002-02 (no data modified); NFR-005; `INV-BE-063`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-rejected-requests-do-not-write` (planned).\n- **API.** all seven.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | POST | `/notes` | `notes` | 201 | 400, 401, 403, 415 | Create a note with mandatory title and body, optional pinned flag (BR-005, BR-018; US-002-01) |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` | GET | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Get a single note by id, including archived notes (US-002-06) |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | PATCH | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404 | Partially edit a note; fields not sent are never overwritten, includes pin/unpin via pinned (BR-006, BR-018; US-002-02) |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | POST | `/notes/{id}/archive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 409 | Archive a note without deleting any data (BR-007; US-002-04) |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | POST | `/notes/{id}/tags` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404 | Add a tag to a note; adding an existing tag creates no duplicate (US-002-03) |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | DELETE | `/notes/{id}/tags/{tag}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Remove a tag from a note (US-002-03) |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | POST | `/notes/{id}/unarchive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Restore an archived note to active with all data preserved (BR-008; US-002-05) |\n\n### `API-NOTES-232390` \u2014 POST `/notes`\n\nCreate a note with mandatory title and body, optional pinned flag (BR-005, BR-018; US-002-01)\n\n**Request:** \n- `title` (string, required): Note title; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n- `body` (string, required): Note body; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n- `pinned` (boolean): Whether the note is pinned; defaults to false (BR-018)\n- `tags` (array): Optional tag names to apply on creation\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `title` (string, required): Note title\n- `body` (string, required): Note body\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived; a new note is active\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-045877` \u2014 GET `/notes/{id}`\n\nGet a single note by id, including archived notes (US-002-06)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `title` (string, required): Note title\n- `body` (string, required): Note body\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-847551` \u2014 PATCH `/notes/{id}`\n\nPartially edit a note; fields not sent are never overwritten, includes pin/unpin via pinned (BR-006, BR-018; US-002-02)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `title` (string): New title; omitted means unchanged\n- `body` (string): New body; omitted means unchanged\n- `pinned` (boolean): Set true to pin, false to unpin; omitted means unchanged\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `title` (string, required): Note title\n- `body` (string, required): Note body\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-810640` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/archive`\n\nArchive a note without deleting any data (BR-007; US-002-04)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `status` (string, required): archived after success\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-359413` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/tags`\n\nAdd a tag to a note; adding an existing tag creates no duplicate (US-002-03)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `tag` (string, required): Tag name to add\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `tags` (array, required): Resulting tag names on the note, without duplicates\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-150096` \u2014 DELETE `/notes/{id}/tags/{tag}`\n\nRemove a tag from a note (US-002-03)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `tag` (string, required): Path parameter: tag name to remove\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `tags` (array, required): Resulting tag names on the note\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-917869` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/unarchive`\n\nRestore an archived note to active with all data preserved (BR-008; US-002-05)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `status` (string, required): active after success\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every operation of this module. `INV-BE-008` and `INV-BE-009` are defined in `backend.domain-model` and apply here unchanged; they are cited, not restated.\n\n- `INV-BE-060`. Every stored note has a non-empty (non-whitespace) `title` and `body`. [BR-005; `ASM-BE-359`, `ASM-BE-360`]\n- `INV-BE-061`. `pinned` is always `true` or `false`, never null. [BR-018; `ASM-BE-034`]\n- `INV-BE-062`. `status` is always `active` or `archived`.\n- `INV-BE-063`. A request that returns a 4xx modifies no stored data. [US-002-01; US-002-02]\n- `INV-BE-064`. A note carries a given tag at most once. [US-002-03]\n- `INV-BE-065`. After archive followed by unarchive, a note's title, body, `pinned` and tag set equal their values before the archive. Neither operation changes any of them. [BR-007; BR-008]\n- `INV-BE-066`. This module never hard-deletes a note row, a tag link made by a successful add, or a tag row. The only deletion it performs is removing a tag link on `API-NOTES-150096`. [BR-007; NFR-005]\n- `INV-BE-067`. `created_at` never changes and `updated_at >= created_at`.\n- `INV-BE-068`. A note's id never changes and is never client-supplied. [`ASM-BE-358`]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nNo input defines roles beyond the actors in `backend.auth-model`. Following `ASM-BE-302`, a role has no permission an input does not grant.\n\n| Operation | Anonymous | Authenticated team member |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` create | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` get | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` edit | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` add tag | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` remove tag | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` archive | deny, 401 | allow |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` unarchive | deny, 401 | allow |\n\n- The allow entries for the team member rest on the default of `Q-BE-001`: a shared workspace where any authenticated member may read and write any note.\n- **Ownership predicate: `BLOCKED`.** No input relates a note to a user (`GAP-BE-003`). BR-031, which carries the note access rule, is corrupted in the supplied inputs. `CON-BE-003` (Critical) questions the shared-workspace default. Until `Q-BE-003` is answered, no ownership column, no access predicate and no 403 for notes is specified. What would unblock it: a stated note access rule.\n- The 401 for a missing or invalid credential depends on the credential form (`Q-BE-012`) and the existing authentication (`GAP-BE-085`).\n- A 403 is documented as part of the response set for authenticated endpoints [US-004-03], but no input names a case that triggers it for notes.\n- Infrastructure actors (Platform/DevOps Engineer, Release/CI Pipeline Owner, Auditor, Engineering Lead) are not API roles for this module. [backend.auth-model]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.jobs | status: UNKNOWN -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work: none specified.** This module defines no sweep, retry or background task.\n\n- **Retention deletion of notes: `BLOCKED`.** NFR-019 as supplied reads \"notes 1 days\", which would delete every note after one day. That conflicts with BR-007, BR-008 and NFR-005 (`CON-BE-300`). Whether it is intended is `Q-BE-300`. The mechanism is `GAP-BE-101`. No note deletion is specified here until `Q-BE-300` is answered.\n- **Retries.** None. Writes are single transactions; the server does not retry a failed write.\n- **Idempotency.**\n  - `API-NOTES-359413` is naturally idempotent: the `(note_id, tag_name)` primary key plus `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` means repeated or concurrent calls leave exactly one link.\n  - `API-NOTES-150096` leaves the same end state when repeated, but a repeat returns 404 because the tag is no longer on the note (`ASM-BE-364`).\n  - `API-NOTES-810640` and `API-NOTES-917869` reach the same end state when repeated, but a repeat returns a conflict (US-002-04).\n  - `API-NOTES-847551` repeats with the same payload produce the same stored fields.\n  - `API-NOTES-232390` is not idempotent. No idempotency key is supplied by any input, so a retried create makes a second note (`GAP-BE-568`).\n- **Concurrency control.** Last-write-wins on supplied fields (`ASM-BE-365`, `Q-BE-353`). No optimistic-lock version column is added.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll names are proposals (planned). Test names under each requirement are listed in `backend.notes.requirements`.\n\n**Unit.**\n- Payload validation: missing, null, empty and whitespace-only `title` and `body`; non-boolean `pinned`; unknown PATCH fields; empty PATCH body (BE-NOTES-FR-003, BE-NOTES-FR-009).\n- PATCH builder emits `SET` only for supplied fields (`test-notes-patch-omitted-fields-untouched`, `INV-BE-008`).\n- State guard: archive allowed only from `active`, unarchive only from `archived`.\n- `updated_at` rule: advanced on any change, not advanced on a duplicate tag add (`ASM-BE-366`).\n\n**Integration** (real PostgreSQL 16, migrations applied from empty):\n- Full lifecycle: create \u2192 edit \u2192 tag \u2192 archive \u2192 get \u2192 unarchive \u2192 get, asserting `INV-BE-065`.\n- Constraint checks: `status` check, non-blank checks, `note_tags` primary key, `ON DELETE RESTRICT`.\n- Concurrency: two simultaneous tag adds of one tag give one link; two simultaneous PATCH requests on different fields keep both fields; two simultaneous archive calls give one success and one conflict.\n- Rejected requests leave the database unchanged (`INV-BE-063`).\n- Authentication: every operation without a credential returns 401. The credential mechanism is `Q-BE-012`, so the test fixture is `BLOCKED` until answered.\n- Search compatibility: a note created, tagged and archived here is visible to the `search` module's read path as defined there (checked in the search module's plan; not re-specified).\n\n**Contract.**\n- Each of the seven operations matches the generated OpenAPI document for its request and response shapes and its documented error statuses [BR-012; NFR-008].\n- Each error response uses the envelope and codes of `backend.error-model`; 415 is never returned by this module.\n- Absence check: no attachment or real-time route exists on the notes paths. The suite is owned by `scope-guardrail` [BR-014; NFR-010].\n- Examples for tagging operations are present in the document [NFR-009].\n\n**Not testable until inputs exist.** Ownership and 403 behaviour (`CON-BE-003`), retention deletion (`CON-BE-300`), and the statuses marked `GAP-BE-309` and `GAP-BE-567`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions\n\nThis module mints no `CON-`. It is affected by two open Critical contradictions and several open items defined in the project spec; they are cited here and not redefined.\n\n### Cited from the project spec\n\n| ID | How it affects this module |\n|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-003` (Critical) | ownership columns and access predicate are `BLOCKED` |\n| `CON-BE-300` (Critical) | note retention deletion is `BLOCKED` |\n| `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`, `Q-BE-300` | shared workspace default, eligibility, credential form, notes retention |\n| `GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-101`, `GAP-BE-300`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-309` | no user\u2013note relation, no PII policy, no field rules, no existing auth, no ORM, no deletion mechanism, no envelope, no encryption statement, open statuses |\n| `ASM-BE-034`, `ASM-BE-302` | `pinned` is `NOT NULL`; no permission unless granted |\n\n### Questions\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default applied |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-351` | degradable | May an archived note be edited, pinned, or have tags changed? | Yes, as for an active note (`ASM-BE-362`). It adds no state and no transition, only a guard, so it does not meet the blocking test. |\n| `Q-BE-352` | degradable | What does unarchive of an already-active note return? | A conflict with `NOTE_NOT_ARCHIVED` (`ASM-BE-363`) |\n| `Q-BE-353` | degradable | Is a concurrent PATCH last-write-wins or a conflict? | Last-write-wins on supplied fields (`ASM-BE-365`) |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | What is missing | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-564` | No operation lists notes. The overview names listing as a responsibility and the module map does not assign it. | whole-notes browsing; `BE-NOTES` has no list requirement | a decision on which module owns list, and its operation record |\n| `GAP-BE-565` | The error code for unarchiving an active note is not in the registry. `NOTE_NOT_ARCHIVED` is a proposed name. | BE-NOTES-FR-017 | an addition to the `backend.error-model` registry |\n| `GAP-BE-566` | US-002-02 says a note's tags can be edited, but the supplied operations add and remove tags separately. Whether PATCH also accepts `tags` is not stated. | BE-NOTES-FR-006, BE-NOTES-FR-009 | a decision; default in `ASM-BE-361` is that PATCH does not touch tags |\n| `GAP-BE-567` | Success status and body of tag removal are not stated (200 with the note, or 204). | BE-NOTES-FR-012 | a status decision |\n| `GAP-BE-568` | No idempotency key or duplicate-create rule for `API-NOTES-232390`. | jobs; client retry behaviour | a rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-569` | Rate-limit class for the seven operations is not stated. | contract completeness | a rate-limit policy |\n| `GAP-BE-570` | No delete operation exists, but US-002-06 refers to \"a deleted ... note\". Whether any note is ever deleted is unstated. | `INV-BE-066`; get 404 wording | an answer to `Q-BE-300` and a delete decision |\n| `GAP-BE-571` | No maximum number of tags per note and no maximum title or body size beyond `GAP-BE-035`. | data model limits | field-level requirements |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-356` | Column types: uuid keys, text strings, boolean, timestamptz. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. | a platform decision (`GAP-BE-089`) |\n| `ASM-BE-357` | A new note has `status` `active`. | US-002-04 archives an \"active, existing note\" and the create criteria carry no status. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-358` | The note id is a server-assigned, opaque uuid, never client-supplied. | AC-002-01-01 says the API returns a unique id. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-359` | A title or body of whitespace only counts as empty. | BR-005 rejects \"empty/null\" payloads. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-360` | The non-empty rule applies to PATCH as well as create. | BR-005 says \"at creation\" only; an edit that blanks a field would lose content, against BR-006's intent. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-361` | PATCH accepts only `title`, `body`, `pinned`. An empty payload or an unknown field is 400. Tags change only through the tag operations. | The supplied operation records list these fields; see `GAP-BE-566`. | `GAP-BE-566` |\n| `ASM-BE-362` | Edit, pin and tag changes on an archived note behave as on an active note. | No input forbids them. | `Q-BE-351` |\n| `ASM-BE-363` | Unarchive of an active note is a conflict with `NOTE_NOT_ARCHIVED`. | Symmetry with US-002-04. | `Q-BE-352` |\n| `ASM-BE-364` | Removing a tag not on the note returns 404 `NOT_FOUND`. | No input states it; consistent with the 404 policy. | a status decision (`GAP-BE-567`) |\n| `ASM-BE-365` | Concurrent PATCH requests are last-write-wins on the fields each supplies. | US-005-03 allows \"a standard last-write/conflict response\". | `Q-BE-353` |\n| `ASM-BE-366` | `updated_at` advances on any real change to title, body, `pinned`, tags or `status`. A duplicate tag add changes nothing and does not advance it. | AC-002-02-01 requires an updated timestamp on edit; no input covers other changes. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-367` | Foreign keys use `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. | The module never deletes notes; BR-007 and NFR-005 forbid data loss. | a platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-368` | A tag row persists after its last note is untagged. | No input states the fate of an unused tag. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-369` | Archive, add tag and remove tag on a missing note return 404 `NOT_FOUND`. | The 404 policy covers edit, unarchive and get; these follow it. | product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-370` | Tag names are compared exactly as supplied (case-sensitive, no trimming or normalisation). | `GAP-BE-035` states no rule. | `GAP-BE-035` |\n| `ASM-BE-371` | `pinned` must be a JSON boolean. No coercion from strings or numbers. | BR-018 calls it boolean. | product owner |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-002-01", "US-002-02", "US-002-03", "US-002-04", "US-002-05", "US-002-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: search\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-003, CON-BE-003, Q-BE-012]\nscope: [API-SEARCH-580505]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `search` module serves one read-only operation, `API-SEARCH-580505`, which searches notes by keyword and by tag. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-003-01 to US-003-06] It owns:\n\n1. Keyword search across note title and body. [stories.md US-003-01]\n2. Filtering by one or more tags. [stories.md US-003-02]\n3. Combining keyword and tag filters in one request. [requirements.md BR-010; stories.md US-003-04]\n4. Pagination with a total count. Unbounded result sets are not permitted. [requirements.md BR-011; stories.md US-003-05]\n5. Excluding archived notes by default, with `include_archived=true` as the opt-in. [requirements.md BR-009; stories.md US-003-06]\n6. Ordering: pinned notes before unpinned notes, then by relevance. [requirements.md BR-019; stories.md US-003-01]\n7. The latency target and the 503 behaviour when the search index is degraded or unavailable. [requirements.md NFR-006, NFR-007; stories.md US-003-03]\n8. Request validation for its own parameters, answered with 400. [stories.md US-003-01 to US-003-06]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Creating, editing, tagging, archiving or unarchiving notes. Those belong to `notes`. Search never writes. [backend.module-map]\n- Fetching a single note by id. That is `notes`. [stories.md US-002-06]\n- The tables of NOTE, TAG and NOTE_TAG. `notes` owns them and `search` only reads them. [backend.module-map]\n- Credential validation. `auth` owns it, and the credential form is `Q-BE-012`. [backend.module-map]\n- The global error envelope, which is `backend.error-model`.\n- The OpenAPI document, its examples and its coverage metric. `api-docs` owns them. The examples for search, including an empty-result example, are required. [requirements.md NFR-009; stories.md US-004-05]\n- The web-app search box (US-006-03), which belongs to the web-app surface.\n\n**Dependencies.** `search \u2192 auth` and `search \u2192 notes` (read-only), exactly as in `backend.module-map`. No other module depends on `search`.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-406` onward, `CON-BE-401`, `GAP-BE-614` onward, `INV-BE-110` onward. It mints no `Q-`, because the run's question budget is exhausted (`GAP-BE-502` and the following `GAP-` entries).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**Tables owned: none.** `search` is read-only over data owned by `notes`. [backend.module-map] The physical table and column names are defined in the `notes` module spec and are not repeated here. This section uses the entity and attribute names of `backend.domain-model`.\n\n**Data read.**\n\n| Entity | Attributes read | Use |\n|---|---|---|\n| NOTE | `id`, `title`, `body`, `status`, `pinned` (plus timestamps if returned) | keyword match, archived filter, pinned ordering |\n| NOTE_TAG | `note_id`, `tag_name` | tag filter |\n| TAG | `name` | tag filter |\n\n[backend.domain-model]\n\n**Request parameters.** Types and the final shape are rendered into the contract by DevAgent. Only the constraints stated in the inputs are listed.\n\n| Parameter | Source | Constraint stated in the inputs | Open |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `q` | US-003-01 | missing or empty gives 400 | the conflict with a tags-only search is `CON-BE-401`; maximum length `GAP-BE-035` |\n| `tags` | US-003-02, US-003-04 | invalid characters give 400 | multi-tag meaning and encoding `GAP-BE-614`; format `GAP-BE-035` |\n| `include_archived` | BR-009; US-003-06 | `true` includes archived notes; an invalid value gives 400 | accepted literals `ASM-BE-410` |\n| `page` | US-003-05 | negative or non-numeric gives 400; a page beyond the results gives 200 and an empty array | numbering base `ASM-BE-409` |\n| `limit` | US-003-05; BR-011 | negative or non-numeric gives 400; default and maximum enforced | values `GAP-BE-500`; over-maximum behaviour `GAP-BE-616` |\n\n**Indexes (proposed, migration 002).** `backend.migration-plan` lists indexes serving keyword and tag lookup and states the index type is not stated. US-003-03 requires \"indexed lookup\" on a dataset of e.g. 100k notes. The indexes below are a proposal (`ASM-BE-412`).\n\n| Index | On | Query it serves |\n|---|---|---|\n| text-search index | NOTE `title` and `body` | the `q` match and relevance rank |\n| btree | NOTE_TAG `(tag_name, note_id)` | the `tags` filter |\n| btree | NOTE `(status, pinned)` | the archived filter and the pinned-first ordering |\n\n**Constraints, nullability, defaults and `ON DELETE`.** None, because the module owns no table.\n\n**PII.** Note titles and bodies are user content and are not classified as PII by any input. Search queries (`q`) may contain user-chosen text. Whether queries are logged is `GAP-BE-501`, and no redaction claim is made.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.state-machine | status: DERIVED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n`search` holds no persistent state and no entity has a status in this module. The note status (active or archived) belongs to `notes`. [backend.module-map] The only states are those of a single request.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `RECEIVED` | `REJECTED` | validation fails | any parameter invalid (`BE-SEARCH-FR-003`, `-005`, `-007`, `-010`, `-013`) | 400, `VALIDATION_ERROR`, no query executed |\n| `RECEIVED` | `REJECTED` | caller not authenticated | credential check fails | 401 or 403, blocked on `Q-BE-012`, `GAP-BE-085` |\n| `RECEIVED` | `EXECUTING` | validation and authentication pass | | query runs |\n| `EXECUTING` | `RESPONDED` | query succeeds | | 200 with the page and the total count; an empty array when nothing matches |\n| `EXECUTING` | `UNAVAILABLE` | search index degraded or unavailable | | 503 with a retry-after header (`BE-SEARCH-FR-016`) |\n\n**Terminal states.** `REJECTED`, `RESPONDED` and `UNAVAILABLE`. A request in a terminal state is never resumed, and a client retry is a new request.\n\n**Illegal transitions.** There is none: every request ends in exactly one terminal state.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nStatus codes follow `backend.error-model`. Error code names other than those cited are proposals (`ASM-BE-301`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-001 Keyword search with pinned-first ordering\n**Given** active notes whose title or body match the keyword,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called with `q`,\n**Then** the matching notes are returned with 200, pinned notes first, then ordered by relevance.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01; BR-019.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-keyword-pinned-first` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** Match semantics and the relevance definition are `GAP-BE-615` (default `ASM-BE-408`). Order within a pinned group is `GAP-BE-310`; a stable final tiebreak is `ASM-BE-411`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-002 No match is not an error\n**Given** no note matches the keyword, or no note carries the tag,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** an empty array is returned with 200.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01; US-003-02; US-003-04 (empty result when the tag filter excludes all keyword matches).\n- **Verification.** `test-search-empty-result-200` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-003 Missing or empty keyword with no tag filter\n**Given** neither a non-empty `q` nor a `tags` filter is supplied,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called,\n**Then** 400 is returned with a validation error and no query is executed.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01; BR-010; US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-no-criteria-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** US-003-01 says a missing or empty `q` gives 400, while US-003-02 returns results for `tags` alone. Reading used: `q` is optional when `tags` is present (`CON-BE-401`). `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-004 Tag filter\n**Given** notes tagged `project-x` exist,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called with `tags=project-x`,\n**Then** only notes carrying that tag are returned with 200.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-tag-filter` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** With several tags, a note must carry all of them (`ASM-BE-406`, `GAP-BE-614`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-005 Invalid tag characters\n**Given** the `tags` parameter contains invalid characters,\n**When** the search is requested,\n**Then** 400 is returned with an error detail.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-tags-invalid-chars-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** Which characters are invalid is `GAP-BE-035`. This requirement cannot be tested until it is answered.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-006 Combined keyword and tag filter\n**Given** `q=budget` and `tags=finance` are both supplied,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** only notes matching both criteria are returned.\n- **Traces to.** BR-010; US-003-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-combined-filters` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-007 Malformed filter combination\n**Given** a malformed filter combination,\n**When** the search is requested,\n**Then** 400 is returned with error details.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-malformed-combination-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** \"Malformed combination\" is not defined (`GAP-BE-617`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-008 Pagination with total count\n**Given** more matches exist than the page size,\n**When** `page` and `limit` are supplied,\n**Then** the requested subset is returned together with the total count of matches.\n- **Traces to.** BR-011; US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pagination-subset-total` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The numbering base is `ASM-BE-409`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-009 Page beyond the results\n**Given** a page number beyond the available results,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** an empty array is returned with 200.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-page-beyond-200` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-010 Invalid page or limit\n**Given** a negative or non-numeric `page` or `limit`,\n**When** the search is requested,\n**Then** 400 is returned with a validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-page-limit-invalid-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-011 Default and maximum page size\n**Given** a request without `limit`, or with a `limit` above the maximum,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** the default page size applies in the first case and the maximum is enforced in the second. No response is unbounded.\n- **Traces to.** BR-011; NFR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-default-and-max-limit` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on the values (`GAP-BE-500`) and on whether an over-maximum `limit` is clamped or rejected (`GAP-BE-616`). NFR-007 names \"page sizes of 100\" only as a latency measurement point.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-012 Archived notes\n**Given** both archived and active notes match,\n**When** a default search is executed,\n**Then** only active notes are returned. With `include_archived=true`, archived notes are included.\n- **Traces to.** BR-009; US-003-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-excludes-archived-default`, `test-search-include-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-013 Invalid `include_archived`\n**Given** an invalid `include_archived` value,\n**When** the search is requested,\n**Then** 400 is returned with an error message.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-include-archived-invalid-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The accepted literals are `ASM-BE-410`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-014 Ordering applies before pagination\n**Given** pinned and unpinned notes matching across several pages,\n**When** pages are requested in sequence,\n**Then** every pinned match appears before every unpinned match across the whole sequence, and no note is repeated or skipped between pages.\n- **Traces to.** BR-019; US-003-01; US-003-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pinned-first-across-pages` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The no-repeat, no-skip part depends on the stable tiebreak `ASM-BE-411`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-015 Latency\n**Given** a load of 50 requests per second,\n**When** searches are executed,\n**Then** p95 latency is under 300 ms, and is maintained up to page sizes of 100, including on a dataset of e.g. 100k notes via indexed lookup.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-006; NFR-007; US-003-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-load-p95-300ms` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The measurement environment is `GAP-BE-621`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-016 Index degraded or unavailable\n**Given** the search index is degraded or unavailable,\n**When** a search is requested,\n**Then** 503 is returned with a retry-after header (error code `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE`, a proposed name) and the request does not hang.\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03; `backend.error-model`.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-index-unavailable-503` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The retry-after value, the definition of \"degraded\" and the timeout are `GAP-BE-618`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-017 Input is never executed\n**Given** any value of `q`, `tags`, `include_archived`, `page` or `limit`,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** the value is treated as data and never as query syntax, so that a security scan finds 0 critical injection vulnerabilities per release.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-injection-strings` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** The method of sanitisation is not stated by any input (`GAP-BE-620`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-018 Authentication required\n**Given** a caller without a valid credential,\n**When** `API-SEARCH-580505` is called,\n**Then** 401 is returned, and 403 where the caller is authenticated but not permitted.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03; `backend.auth-model`.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-unauthenticated-401` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-012` and `GAP-BE-085`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 503 | Search notes by keyword and tags, with pagination and ordering where pinned notes precede unpinned notes |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch notes by keyword and tags, with pagination and ordering where pinned notes precede unpinned notes\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string): Keyword to match against notes. The parameter name is proposed because no input names it. Invalid values return 400 (US-003-01, US-003-02).\n- `tags` (array): Tag names to filter by. The parameter name and encoding are proposed. Tag format is unspecified (GAP-BE-035). A malformed value returns 400 (US-003-02, US-003-04).\n- `include_archived` (boolean): Whether archived notes are included in the results. The parameter name comes from the 400 policy in the error model. An invalid value returns 400 (US-003-04).\n- `page` (integer): Page number for pagination. The name is proposed. An invalid value returns 400 (US-003-05, BR-011).\n- `limit` (integer): Page size. The name is proposed. The default and maximum are unstated (GAP-BE-500); the latency target holds up to page sizes of 100 (NFR-007). An invalid value returns 400 (US-003-05).\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Matching notes for the requested page, in order. Every pinned note precedes every unpinned note (INV-BE-001, BR-019). The order within a group is undefined (GAP-BE-310). Each item shows note fields; their schema is defined by the notes module.\n- `page` (integer, required): The page number returned. The name is proposed (US-003-05).\n- `limit` (integer, required): The page size applied. The name is proposed (US-003-05).\n- `total` (integer): Total number of matching notes across all pages. The field is proposed, and no input requires it (US-003-05).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-001` (cited from `backend.domain-model`). In search results, every pinned note precedes every unpinned note. [requirements.md BR-019]\n- `INV-BE-110`. `API-SEARCH-580505` performs no write to any table. After every call, the stored notes, tags and note-tag links are unchanged. [backend.module-map: read-only access]\n- `INV-BE-111`. Unless `include_archived=true` was supplied and valid, no returned note has the archived status. [requirements.md BR-009]\n- `INV-BE-112`. When both `q` and `tags` are supplied, every returned note satisfies both. [stories.md US-003-04]\n- `INV-BE-113`. A response that returns results never exceeds the maximum page size. [requirements.md BR-011] The value is `GAP-BE-500`.\n- `INV-BE-114`. A request with an invalid parameter never receives a 200 response. [stories.md US-003-01 to US-003-06]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nNo input defines roles beyond what `backend.auth-model` records, and following `ASM-BE-302` no permission exists unless an input grants it.\n\n| Operation | Anonymous | Authenticated team member |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | deny | allow |\n\n[backend.auth-model: `GET /notes/search`]\n\n- The allow entry rests on `Q-BE-001` (shared workspace default).\n- **Ownership predicate: `BLOCKED`.** Whether a member sees all notes or only their own is decided by `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003` (Critical). If a per-owner rule is chosen, this module must add an owner filter to every query and the `total` count must reflect only the visible notes. No ownership column is assumed.\n- Denial uses 401 (no valid credential) and 403 (authenticated but not permitted). [stories.md US-004-03] The credential check itself is `auth`'s (`Q-BE-012`, `GAP-BE-085`).\n- The infrastructure actors in `backend.auth-model` hold no role on this operation.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work owned by this module: none stated.** No input describes a periodic task for search.\n- **Retries.** None are performed by the server. A client may retry after a 503, using the retry-after header. [stories.md US-003-03]\n- **Idempotency.** `GET` is safe and idempotent, so no idempotency key applies and no state changes between repeated calls.\n- **Index maintenance.** Whether the search index is a database index or a separate component, and how it stays consistent with note writes (including archive and tag changes), is not stated in any input (`GAP-BE-618`). No refresh job or lag figure is claimed.\n- **Retention.** The retention sweep of NFR-019 does not touch data this module reads. The note-deletion reading of NFR-019 is `CON-BE-300`, owned by `notes`. If notes are ever deleted, search results must follow, which is a dependency on that decision.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n\n- Parameter validation: `q`, `tags`, `include_archived`, `page`, `limit`, each valid, invalid, absent. (`test-search-no-criteria-400`, `test-search-page-limit-invalid-400`, `test-search-include-archived-invalid-400`, `test-search-tags-invalid-chars-400`, `test-search-malformed-combination-400`)\n- Ordering comparator: pinned before unpinned, then relevance, then the stable tiebreak. (`test-search-pinned-first-across-pages`)\n- Injection strings in every parameter. (`test-search-injection-strings`)\n\n**Integration** (against PostgreSQL 16 [platform.md]).\n\n- Keyword match, empty result, tag filter, combined filter. (`test-search-keyword-pinned-first`, `test-search-empty-result-200`, `test-search-tag-filter`, `test-search-combined-filters`)\n- Archived default and `include_archived=true`. (`test-search-excludes-archived-default`, `test-search-include-archived`)\n- Pagination: subset plus total, page beyond results, default and maximum limit. (`test-search-pagination-subset-total`, `test-search-page-beyond-200`, `test-search-default-and-max-limit`)\n- Read-only check: row counts and checksums unchanged after calls (`INV-BE-110`).\n- Index unavailable simulated: 503 and retry-after. (`test-search-index-unavailable-503`)\n- Authentication: 401 without a credential. (`test-search-unauthenticated-401`) Blocked on `Q-BE-012`.\n\n**Load.**\n\n- 50 requests per second, p95 under 300 ms, with page size up to 100 and a dataset of e.g. 100k notes. (`test-search-load-p95-300ms`) The environment is `GAP-BE-621`.\n\n**Contract.**\n\n- The published OpenAPI document contains `API-SEARCH-580505` with its 400, 401, 403 and 503 responses, an example request with query parameters, an example response body and an example empty-result response. [stories.md US-004-03, US-004-05; requirements.md NFR-008, NFR-009] Examples must carry no real-looking PII.\n- The scope-guardrail suite finds no excluded category among the module's paths. [requirements.md NFR-010]\n\n**Test data.** Fixtures needed: pinned and unpinned, active and archived notes, notes with several tags, a notes set of e.g. 100k for load. No value is supplied by any input beyond the examples in the stories (`project-x`, `budget`, `finance`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n**Contradiction check (core rule 8).**\n\n- Story to design: the design_brief contains acceptance criteria for US-001-01 to US-001-06 and US-002-01 only. None cover search, so there is nothing to contradict. Nothing found.\n- Story to requirement: BR-009, BR-010, BR-011 and BR-019 agree with US-003-01 to US-003-06. Nothing found.\n- Requirement to requirement and story to story: `CON-BE-401`. Also `GAP-BE-310` (BR-019 against the relevance ordering of US-003-01), recorded in the project spec.\n- Design to design: nothing found.\n\n### Contradiction\n\n| ID | Severity | Statement |\n|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-401` | non-critical | US-003-01 says a missing or empty `q` returns 400. US-003-02 returns notes for `tags=project-x` with no `q`, and BR-010 says keyword and tag filters may be combined. If US-003-01 is read strictly, a tags-only search is impossible. If US-003-02 is read as stated, 400 applies only when neither `q` nor `tags` is given. Reading used: `q` is optional when `tags` is supplied, because it satisfies both US-003-02 and BR-010. It touches no data shape, state machine, authorisation rule or money/PII path. Affected: `BE-SEARCH-FR-003` (`DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`). |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Statement | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-614` | The meaning of several tags (all or any), how they are encoded in `tags`, and whether tag matching is case-sensitive are not stated. US-003-02 says \"one or more tags\". | `BE-SEARCH-FR-004`, `-006` | field-level requirements for the `tags` parameter |\n| `GAP-BE-615` | Keyword match semantics are not stated: case handling, word splitting, partial words, and what \"relevance\" ranks by. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-001` | a search-behaviour rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-616` | Whether `limit` above the maximum is clamped or rejected with 400 is not stated. BR-011 says only that a maximum is enforced. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-011` | a pagination decision (with `GAP-BE-500`) |\n| `GAP-BE-617` | \"Malformed filter combination\" (US-003-04) is not defined. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-007` | a list of invalid combinations |\n| `GAP-BE-618` | What counts as a degraded or unavailable index, the retry-after value, the timeout, and how the index is kept consistent with note writes are not stated. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-016`, `jobs` | an operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-619` | No rate-limit class for the search operation is stated. | `api-contract` | a rate-limit decision |\n| `GAP-BE-620` | NFR-012 gives a target, not a sanitisation method. No sanitisation rule for `q` and `tags` is stated. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-017` | a security requirement |\n| `GAP-BE-621` | No environment, hardware or dataset-shape is stated for the p95 and 50 requests per second target, and whether it is measured at the server or the client. | `BE-SEARCH-FR-015` | a performance-test definition |\n\nOpen gaps defined in the project spec and relevant here: `GAP-BE-310` (order within a group), `GAP-BE-500` (page sizes), `GAP-BE-035` (tag and query format, length), `GAP-BE-085` (existing authentication), `GAP-BE-501` (logging), `GAP-BE-003` (note-to-user relation). Blocking items: `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003` (ownership predicate), `Q-BE-012` (credential).\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-406` | Several tags combine as AND: a note must carry every listed tag. | US-003-02 says filters \"narrow results\" and US-003-04 returns notes \"matching both criteria\". Narrowing is the narrower reading. | answer to `GAP-BE-614` |\n| `ASM-BE-407` | `tags` is a single parameter holding a comma-separated list. | No input states an encoding. | answer to `GAP-BE-614` |\n| `ASM-BE-408` | The keyword match is case-insensitive over title and body, and relevance is the rank produced by the database text-search facility. | US-003-01 says \"across title and content\" and \"ordered by relevance\"; nothing defines either. | answer to `GAP-BE-615` |\n| `ASM-BE-409` | `page` starts at 1. | No input states a base. `page=0` would then give 400. | answer to `GAP-BE-616` |\n| `ASM-BE-410` | `include_archived` accepts the literals `true` and `false` only; any other value gives 400. | US-003-06 requires 400 for an invalid value and names `true`. | answer to `GAP-BE-616` |\n| `ASM-BE-411` | The final sort key after pinned and relevance is the note `id`, so results are deterministic. | Pagination (US-003-05) needs a total order, and `GAP-BE-310` leaves the within-group order undefined. | answer to `GAP-BE-310` |\n| `ASM-BE-412` | The indexes proposed in the data model (text-search on title and body, btree on tag name and note id, btree on status and pinned) are used. | US-003-03 requires indexed lookup; the type is unstated (`backend.migration-plan`). | a platform decision (`GAP-BE-089`) |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: residency-compliance\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-151, Q-BE-152, CON-BE-151, GAP-BE-303, GAP-BE-305]\nscope: [API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The module evaluates and records EU data residency for notes_api infrastructure (database, compute, backup storage). [backend.module-map; BR-001 to BR-004; NFR-001 to NFR-004] It owns:\n\n1. Validating a proposed resource region, including a backup destination, against the approved EU region list, with a rejection decision (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`). [US-001-01, US-001-02; AC-001-01-02, AC-001-02-02]\n2. The CI/CD residency gate: evaluating a deployment's resource regions. Missing or unreadable region metadata is a failure, never a default pass (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907`). [US-001-03; BR-002; `INV-BE-007`]\n3. Scanning the INFRA_RESOURCE inventory and assigning each resource `pass`, `fail` or `unverified` (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`). A resource type the scanner does not recognise is flagged `unverified`, never omitted. [US-001-04; AC-001-04-02]\n4. The compliance report with an overall percent-compliant figure (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`). [US-001-04; NFR-004; AC-001-04-01]\n5. Evaluating the launch gate from the report (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`). [US-001-05; BR-003] What a passed gate authorises is `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`.\n6. Periodic post-launch scanning and drift alerts (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`). [US-001-06; BR-004; NFR-003]\n7. Owning the data of INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT, plus the scan, deployment-check and approved-region records proposed in section 2. [backend.domain-model]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Provisioning, modifying or deleting any infrastructure. BR-001 names infrastructure-as-code region constraints as its enforcement, and that lives outside this service. This module only decides and records. (`INV-BE-166`)\n- The notes and login data (`notes`, `auth`), the global error envelope (`backend.error-model`), and logging (`backend.observability`).\n- Alert delivery tooling and recipients beyond \"the DevOps team\" (`GAP-BE-308`).\n- The Engineering Lead's launch sign-off process, until `Q-BE-152` is answered.\n- The approved region list's content. It is supplied, not invented (`Q-BE-151`).\n\n**Operations owned.** The seven ids in the frontmatter, referenced by id only.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block. All other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nNo input states column types or the location of this data (`GAP-BE-305`). PostgreSQL 16 is the platform [platform.md]. The tables below are a proposal that keeps the data in the notes_api database, with uuid keys, text, `timestamptz`, integer and jsonb types (`ASM-BE-456`).\n\n**approved_regions**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `region_code` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; stored exactly as supplied (`ASM-BE-461`) |\n| `added_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nContent is `Q-BE-151`. While empty, no region is approved (`ASM-BE-462`).\n\n**infra_resources**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `external_ref` | text | NOT NULL | none | unique; the resource's identity in the infrastructure (`GAP-BE-664`) |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | none | no CHECK list, so unrecognised types can be stored (`ASM-BE-458`) |\n| `region` | text | NULL | none | null means region metadata missing or unreadable |\n| `compliance_status` | text | NOT NULL | `'unverified'` | `CHECK (compliance_status IN ('pass','fail','unverified'))` [US-001-04] |\n| `status_reason` | text | NULL | none | |\n| `last_scanned_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | null until first scanned |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nIndexes: unique on `external_ref` (resource identity); btree on `compliance_status` (serves the status filter in `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` and the report counts).\n\n**residency_scans**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `scan_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `trigger` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (trigger IN ('manual','periodic'))` |\n| `outcome` | text | NOT NULL | `'running'` | `CHECK (outcome IN ('running','completed','failed'))` |\n| `started_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `completed_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | set when outcome leaves `running` |\n| `resources_total` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_total >= 0)` |\n| `resources_pass` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_pass >= 0)` |\n| `resources_fail` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_fail >= 0)` |\n| `resources_unverified` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_unverified >= 0)` |\n\nIndexes: partial unique index on `(outcome)` where `outcome = 'running'`, which enforces one running scan (`INV-BE-165`); btree on `(started_at DESC)`, which serves \"latest completed scan\" for the report and the gate.\n\n**deployment_checks**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `check_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `evaluated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `outcome` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (outcome IN ('passed','failed'))` |\n| `detail` | jsonb | NULL | none | resources evaluated with region and per-resource result |\n\nIndex: btree on `(evaluated_at DESC)`, which serves audit lookup. The table is an audit record only; the check result is computed from the request.\n\n**drift_alerts**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `alert_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | FK `infra_resources`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `scan_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | FK `residency_scans`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `detected_region` | text | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `raised_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `delivery_status` | text | NOT NULL | `'pending'` | `CHECK (delivery_status IN ('pending','delivered','failed'))` |\n| `delivered_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK (delivered_at IS NULL OR delivered_at >= raised_at)` |\n\nIndexes: btree on `(raised_at DESC)`, which serves the alert list; btree on `(resource_id, raised_at DESC)`, which serves \"previous alert for this resource\".\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`.\n\n**PII.** None of these tables holds personal data in any input. No encryption-at-rest statement exists, so none is made (`GAP-BE-304`).\n\n**Not stored.** The launch-gate result is derived from the latest completed scan and is not persisted (`ASM-BE-463`). A sign-off record is `GAP-BE-672`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n### 3.1 Resource compliance status (`infra_resources.compliance_status`)\n\nStates: `unverified` (initial), `pass`, `fail`. None is terminal; every scan re-evaluates. [US-001-04, US-001-06]\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `unverified` | resource first recorded | not yet scanned |\n| any | `pass` | scan evaluates the resource | type recognised (`ASM-BE-458`), region non-null, region in `approved_regions` (`ASM-BE-461`) |\n| any | `fail` | scan evaluates the resource | type recognised and region non-null but not in `approved_regions`; or region null or unreadable (`ASM-BE-459`) |\n| any | `unverified` | scan evaluates the resource | type not recognised [AC-001-04-02] |\n\nEvaluation order for one resource: unrecognised type first, then missing region, then membership of the approved list. Illegal transitions do not exist, because status is only written by a scan.\n\n### 3.2 Scan (`residency_scans.outcome`)\n\nStates: `running` (initial), `completed` (terminal), `failed` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (new) | `running` | manual trigger or periodic job | no other scan `running` (`INV-BE-165`) |\n| `running` | `completed` | every resource evaluated | counts partition the total (`INV-BE-167`) |\n| `running` | `failed` | the scan cannot finish | reasons undecided (`GAP-BE-664`) |\n\nA trigger while a scan is running is refused. The status and code are `GAP-BE-667`.\n\n### 3.3 Deployment check (`deployment_checks.outcome`)\n\nStates: `passed` (terminal), `failed` (terminal). Created in its final state in one step. [US-001-03]\n\n| Outcome | Condition |\n|---|---|\n| `passed` | at least one resource supplied (`ASM-BE-466`) and every resource has a readable region in `approved_regions` |\n| `failed` | any resource has a missing or unreadable region, or a non-approved region, or none was supplied [AC-001-03-02, AC-001-03-03] |\n\n### 3.4 Drift alert\n\nA drift alert is raised, not transitioned. It is created when a scan moves a resource into `fail` on a non-approved region, or finds a failing resource with no earlier alert for that region (`ASM-BE-460`). `delivery_status` goes `pending` to `delivered` or `failed`. Delivered is terminal. `failed` may be retried (`GAP-BE-668`). No acknowledge or resolve state is defined by any input (`GAP-BE-668`).\n\n### 3.5 Launch gate (derived, no storage)\n\n`PASSED` when the latest completed scan has `resources_total > 0` and every resource is `pass` (100% EU-verified). `FAILED` otherwise. [US-001-05; AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02; `ASM-BE-457`] With no completed scan the gate is `FAILED` (`ASM-BE-463`). What `PASSED` authorises is `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-001 Approve an EU region\n**Given** a proposed resource region that is in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** the request is accepted as compliant.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; AC-001-01-01; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-eu-accepts` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`.\n- **Status.** The list content is `Q-BE-151`. Until it is supplied nothing can be accepted (`ASM-BE-462`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-002 Reject a non-EU region\n**Given** a proposed region that is not in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** the request is rejected and no `infra_resources` row is created.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; AC-001-01-02; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-noneu-rejects-no-write` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`. Status and code: `GAP-BE-667`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-003 Validate a backup destination quickly\n**Given** a backup destination outside `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` validates it before a backup runs,\n**Then** the validation fails within 30 seconds with a residency violation error and no data is written.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-backup-validation-30s` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`. The start of the 30 seconds is `GAP-BE-673`. A backup destination within the EU passes as in FR-001 (AC-001-02-01 is satisfied by the destination being validated; the backup job itself is out of scope).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-004 Deployment gate passes\n**Given** a deployment whose evaluated resources all have a readable region in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` is called,\n**Then** the check passes, an audit row with outcome `passed` is stored, and the deployment may proceed.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-01; BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-deploycheck-pass` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-005 Deployment gate fails on a non-EU resource\n**Given** at least one resource outside `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` is called,\n**Then** the check fails, an audit row with outcome `failed` is stored, and the deployment is blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-02; BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-deploycheck-fail-noneu` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-006 Deployment gate fails closed on missing region\n**Given** a resource whose region metadata is missing or unreadable (or no resource is supplied, `ASM-BE-466`),\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` is called,\n**Then** the check fails and blocks the deployment, never passing by default.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-03; `INV-BE-007`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-deploycheck-missing-region-fails`, `test-residency-deploycheck-empty-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-007 Scan classifies every resource\n**Given** recorded `infra_resources`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` runs a scan,\n**Then** each resource gets `pass`, `fail` or `unverified` per section 3.1, `last_scanned_at` is set, and the scan row stores counts that partition the total.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; NFR-001; AC-001-04-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-classifies-all`, `test-residency-scan-counts-partition` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`. How resources and regions are discovered is `GAP-BE-664`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-008 Unrecognised type is flagged\n**Given** a resource whose type the scanner does not recognise,\n**When** a scan runs or the list or report is read,\n**Then** the resource appears with status `unverified` and is never omitted.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-02; `INV-BE-161`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-unknown-type-unverified` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-009 List resources\n**Given** recorded resources,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` is called,\n**Then** each resource is returned with its region and compliance status.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; US-001-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-list-resources` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`. Pagination, sorting and filtering are decided in the contract rendering (the project spec states no page size, `GAP-BE-500`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-010 Compliance report\n**Given** a completed scan,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` is called,\n**Then** the report lists every resource with its region and pass/fail/unverified status, plus an overall percent-compliant metric computed per `ASM-BE-457`, and states the scan it was derived from.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; NFR-004; AC-001-04-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-lists-all-with-percent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`. Whether the report is refreshed at each gate check: `GAP-BE-666`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-011 Launch gate evaluation\n**Given** the latest completed scan,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate is `PASSED` only when 100% of resources are EU-verified and `FAILED` otherwise, and the scan it used is identified.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-pass-at-100`, `test-residency-gate-fail-below-100`, `test-residency-gate-fail-no-scan` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` for any statement that `PASSED` authorises release: `CON-BE-151` (Critical), `Q-BE-152`. Only the evaluation is specified.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-012 Drift alert\n**Given** a periodic scan finds a resource in a region not in `approved_regions`,\n**When** the scan completes that resource,\n**Then** a drift alert with the resource and detected region is created and delivery to the DevOps team begins at once.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-drift-alert-raised` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`. Timing: `CON-BE-451`. Channel: `GAP-BE-308`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-013 No alert when compliant\n**Given** a periodic scan in which every resource remains in `approved_regions`,\n**When** the scan completes,\n**Then** no alert is raised and the scan records a pass (outcome `completed` with no failing resources).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-clean-no-alert` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-014 List drift alerts\n**Given** raised alerts,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` is called,\n**Then** alerts are returned with resource details, detected region and raise time.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-list-drift-alerts` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` | POST | `/residency/deployment-checks` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | CI/CD residency gate: evaluate a deployment's resource regions; missing or unreadable region metadata fails the check and blocks deployment (US-001-03, INV-BE-007). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` | GET | `/residency/drift-alerts` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | List DRIFT_ALERT records raised when a resource is detected outside the approved EU regions after launch (US-001-06, NFR-003). Alert channel is unresolved (GAP-BE-308). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | GET | `/residency/launch-gate` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403 | Evaluate the launch gate from the current compliance report (US-001-05). Whether a pass authorises release or needs Engineering Lead sign-off is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` | GET | `/residency/report` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Retrieve the compliance report: EU verification status of every infrastructure component, refreshed at each launch gate check (US-001-04, NFR-004). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` | GET | `/residency/resources` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | List INFRA_RESOURCE records with region and compliance status (pass, fail, unverified) (US-001-01, US-001-04). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` | POST | `/residency/scans` | `residency-compliance` | 202 | 401, 403, 409 | Trigger a residency scan of INFRA_RESOURCE records, flagging unrecognised resource types as unverified (US-001-04). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` | POST | `/residency/validations` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | Validate a proposed resource region against the approved EU region list (US-001-01, US-001-02). Path is proposed; no input supplies it (GAP-BE-303). Region list pending Q-BE-151. |\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` \u2014 POST `/residency/deployment-checks`\n\nCI/CD residency gate: evaluate a deployment's resource regions; missing or unreadable region metadata fails the check and blocks deployment (US-001-03, INV-BE-007). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `deployment_id` (string, required): Identifier of the pipeline deployment being checked.\n- `resources` (array, required): Resources to check, each with resource_type and region metadata.\n**Response:** \n- `passed` (boolean, required): True only if every resource is in an approved EU region.\n- `failures` (array, required): Resources that failed, with region and reason, including missing metadata.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` \u2014 GET `/residency/drift-alerts`\n\nList DRIFT_ALERT records raised when a resource is detected outside the approved EU regions after launch (US-001-06, NFR-003). Alert channel is unresolved (GAP-BE-308).
