Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_id` (string): Filter alerts by resource.\n- `raised_since` (string): Only alerts raised at or after this timestamp.\n- `page` (integer): Page number; page size undefined (GAP-BE-500).\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Alerts with id, resource_id, detected_region, raised_at.\n- `total` (integer): Total matching alerts.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` \u2014 GET `/residency/launch-gate`\n\nEvaluate the launch gate from the current compliance report (US-001-05). Whether a pass authorises release or needs Engineering Lead sign-off is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `gate_passed` (boolean, required): Whether the report shows all components compliant.\n- `blocking_resources` (array, required): Resources preventing launch; empty when passed.\n- `evaluated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of evaluation, after report refresh.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` \u2014 GET `/residency/report`\n\nRetrieve the compliance report: EU verification status of every infrastructure component, refreshed at each launch gate check (US-001-04, NFR-004). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp the report was refreshed.\n- `resources` (array, required): Each component with region, compliance status and last scan time.\n- `summary` (object, required): Counts of pass, fail and unverified components.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` \u2014 GET `/residency/resources`\n\nList INFRA_RESOURCE records with region and compliance status (pass, fail, unverified) (US-001-01, US-001-04). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_type` (string): Filter by resource type.\n- `compliance_status` (string): Filter by pass, fail or unverified.\n- `page` (integer): Page number; page size undefined (GAP-BE-500).\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Resources with resource_id, resource_type, region, compliance_status, last_scanned_at.\n- `total` (integer): Total matching resources.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` \u2014 POST `/residency/scans`\n\nTrigger a residency scan of INFRA_RESOURCE records, flagging unrecognised resource types as unverified (US-001-04). Path is proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_types` (array): Optional restriction of the scan to given resource types.\n**Response:** \n- `scan_id` (string, required): Identifier of the started scan.\n- `status` (string, required): Scan state at acceptance.\n- `started_at` (string, required): Timestamp the scan was accepted.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` \u2014 POST `/residency/validations`\n\nValidate a proposed resource region against the approved EU region list (US-001-01, US-001-02). Path is proposed; no input supplies it (GAP-BE-303). Region list pending Q-BE-151.\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_type` (string, required): Kind of component: database, compute or backup storage.\n- `region` (string, required): Region identifier to validate.\n- `resource_id` (string): Identifier of an existing INFRA_RESOURCE being validated, if any.\n**Response:** \n- `compliant` (boolean, required): Whether the region is an approved EU region.\n- `region` (string, required): The region that was evaluated.\n- `reason` (string): Explanation when the region is rejected.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-160`. A resource has `compliance_status = 'pass'` only if, at its last scan, its type was recognised, its region was non-null, and its region was in `approved_regions`. [BR-001; US-001-04]\n- `INV-BE-161`. Every `infra_resources` row appears in the list and in the report; a resource of an unrecognised type appears as `unverified`. [AC-001-04-02]\n- `INV-BE-162`. A deployment check is `passed` only if at least one resource was evaluated and every one has a readable region in `approved_regions`. This restates `INV-BE-007` for the check outcome. [AC-001-03-03]\n- `INV-BE-163`. Percent compliant is `pass` count divided by total count, with `unverified` in the denominator. The gate is `PASSED` iff the latest completed scan has a non-zero total and 100% `pass`. [US-001-05; `ASM-BE-457`]\n- `INV-BE-164`. Each drift alert references an existing resource and a `detected_region` that was not in `approved_regions` when it was raised. [BR-004]\n- `INV-BE-165`. At most one scan has outcome `running`. (partial unique index)\n- `INV-BE-166`. A rejected validation, and any other operation of this module, creates, changes or deletes no infrastructure and no `infra_resources` row except through a scan. [AC-001-01-02; purpose]\n- `INV-BE-167`. For a completed scan, `resources_pass + resources_fail + resources_unverified = resources_total`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**BLOCKED.** The matrix cannot be written. The project spec records that the infrastructure actors in the stories are not API roles in any input, and that the existing authentication is unnamed (`GAP-BE-085`). It also leaves access to the compliance report undecided. Following `ASM-BE-302`, no actor is granted any operation until an input grants it.\n\n**Actors named in the stories, with the operations their stories imply** (evidence only, not a grant):\n\n| Operation | Actor named in the story |\n|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` | Platform/DevOps Engineer [US-001-01, US-001-02] |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` | Release/CI Pipeline Owner [US-001-03] |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` | Compliance/Security Auditor [US-001-04] |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | Engineering Lead [US-001-05] |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` | Platform/DevOps Engineer [US-001-06] |\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None: the module's data is not user-scoped, and no input relates it to a user.\n\n**What would unblock this.** A description of the existing authentication and its roles (`GAP-BE-085`), and a decision on how the pipeline authenticates to these operations (`GAP-BE-669`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Periodic residency scan.** Runs the same scan as `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` with trigger `periodic`. The period is no longer than 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-153`, from NFR-003), exact value `Q-BE-153`. The scheduler and where it runs are `GAP-BE-665`.\n- *Idempotency / concurrency.* The partial unique index on `running` scans means a second start while one runs is a no-op for the job (`INV-BE-165`). No client-supplied idempotency key is specified.\n- *Retry.* A scan that ends `failed` is retried on the next period. No other retry policy is stated (`GAP-BE-664`).\n\n**Alert delivery.** Starts when an alert row is created. Retry, backoff and failure handling are `GAP-BE-668`. The recipients and channel are `GAP-BE-308`.\n\n**Retention.** No retention period is stated for scans, checks or alerts (`GAP-BE-671`). No sweep is specified.\n\n**Evaluate-only operations.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` and `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` have no side effect beyond the audit row of the latter, so repeating a call is safe (`ASM-BE-465`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n- Region membership: exact match, case-sensitive, no normalisation; empty list approves nothing. (`ASM-BE-461`, `ASM-BE-462`)\n- Resource classification order (section 3.1) and the pass/fail/unverified outcomes.\n- Percent-compliant and gate rule, including zero resources and no completed scan. (`INV-BE-163`)\n- Drift alert raise rule (`ASM-BE-460`).\n- Injectable clock for the 30-second backup validation bound.\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16).\n- Scan end to end: counts partition the total (`INV-BE-167`); only one running scan (`INV-BE-165`); a concurrent trigger is refused.\n- Rejected validation writes no resource row (`INV-BE-166`).\n- Unrecognised type is listed and reported as `unverified` (`INV-BE-161`).\n- Deployment check audit rows for pass, fail, missing region and empty input.\n- Periodic job produces no alert on a clean scan and an alert on a drifted resource.\n- Foreign keys use `ON DELETE RESTRICT`.\n\n**Contract.**\n- Each of the seven operation ids appears in the published OpenAPI document with its documented errors (BR-012, NFR-008).\n- The compliance report operation is covered by the coverage-check.\n- Error statuses are asserted once `GAP-BE-667` is decided.\n\n**Not testable yet.** Region list content (`Q-BE-151`), launch authorisation (`Q-BE-152`), alert delivery (`GAP-BE-308`), authorization (section 7).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions\n\n### Open items cited from the project spec\n`Q-BE-151` (blocks FR-001 to FR-006), `Q-BE-152` and `CON-BE-151` (Critical; block the authorisation meaning of FR-011), `Q-BE-153`, `ASM-BE-153`, `GAP-BE-303` (paths), `GAP-BE-305` (storage location), `GAP-BE-307`, `GAP-BE-308`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-500`, `INV-BE-007`, `ASM-BE-302`.\n\n### Contradictions\n\n| ID | Severity | Statement |\n|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-451` | non-critical | AC-001-06-02 says the alert is sent \"immediately\", NFR-003 says \"within 15 minutes of drift detection\". Reading used: delivery starts at once on detection, and 15 minutes is the upper bound (strongest citation, NFR-003). |\n\n### Questions (degradable, defaults taken in an unattended run)\n\n| ID | Class | Question and default |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-451` | degradable | Should the launch gate require a scan newer than some age? Default: no age limit; the response identifies the scan used (`ASM-BE-463`). |\n| `Q-BE-452` | degradable | Is a resource with a missing region in a scan `fail` or `unverified`? Default: `fail` (`ASM-BE-459`). |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Statement and reason |\n|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-456` | Types are uuid keys, text, timestamptz, integer, jsonb, and the data lives in the notes_api database. Reason: no types or location are supplied (`GAP-BE-305`); PostgreSQL 16 is the platform. |\n| `ASM-BE-457` | Percent compliant is `pass` over the total, with `unverified` counted as not verified; zero resources is not 100%. Reason: US-001-05 asks for \"100% EU-verified\" and AC-001-04-02 says unverified is not silently omitted. |\n| `ASM-BE-458` | Recognised resource types are database, compute and backup storage; any other is unrecognised. Reason: BR-001 names database, compute and backups. |\n| `ASM-BE-459` | A resource with a null or unreadable region is `fail` in a scan. Reason: AC-001-03-03 fails closed for the deployment check; the scan case is not stated (`Q-BE-452`). |\n| `ASM-BE-460` | A drift alert is raised when a resource enters `fail` on a non-approved region, or is found failing with no earlier alert for that region; later scans of an unchanged failing resource raise none. Reason: BR-004 alerts on detection; repeating the alert every scan is not stated. |\n| `ASM-BE-461` | Region codes are compared exactly as supplied, case-sensitive, with no normalisation. Reason: no input states a rule; supplied values are carried verbatim. |\n| `ASM-BE-462` | An empty `approved_regions` approves nothing. Reason: BR-001 allows only approved regions, so absence of a list must not approve any. |\n| `ASM-BE-463` | The gate evaluates the latest completed scan, reports its identity and time, and is `FAILED` when no scan has completed. Reason: US-001-05 reads the compliance report; NFR-004 asks it to be refreshed at each check but states no mechanism (`GAP-BE-666`). |\n| `ASM-BE-465` | `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` and `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-585907` are repeatable. Reason: they are decisions over their input. |\n| `ASM-BE-466` | A deployment check with no resource supplied fails. Reason: nothing could be verified, and AC-001-03-03 forbids passing by default. |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-664` | How resources and their regions are discovered, how a scan can fail, and the unique identity of a resource (`external_ref`) are not stated. | FR-007, scan state machine | a platform decision on the inventory source |\n| `GAP-BE-665` | Scheduler and host for the periodic scan are not stated. | jobs | an operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-666` | Whether a gate check refreshes the report, and the freshness age, are not stated. | FR-010, FR-011 | a rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-667` | Statuses and error codes of rejection, concurrent-scan refusal and gate result are not stated. | FR-002, section 3.2 | a status decision for `backend.error-model` |\n| `GAP-BE-668` | Alert delivery retry, deduplication and acknowledge/resolve lifecycle are not stated. | FR-012, section 3.4 | an operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-669` | How the CI pipeline and other callers authenticate to these operations is not stated. | authorization | an authentication description (`GAP-BE-085`) |\n| `GAP-BE-670` | What happens to an `infra_resources` row when the real resource is removed is not stated. | data-model | an operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-671` | Retention of scans, checks and alerts is not stated. | jobs | a data-retention policy |\n| `GAP-BE-672` | Where the Engineering Lead's sign-off is recorded, if required, is not stated. | FR-011 | answer to `Q-BE-152` |\n| `GAP-BE-673` | The instant from which the 30-second bound in AC-001-02-02 is measured is not stated. | FR-003 | a timing definition |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: api-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: [API-APIDOCS-098363, API-APIDOCS-827943]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `api-docs` module publishes the machine-readable description of notes_api and measures how complete it is. It owns:\n\n1. **The published OpenAPI document** (`API-APIDOCS-098363`).\n   - It must list every implemented endpoint: CRUD, tagging, search, archive, and email one-time-code login (send and verify). [stories.md US-004-01; requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008, NFR-016]\n   - It also lists the two operations this module owns, because BR-012 allows no exception.\n2. **The content duties of that document.**\n   - Request and response schemas, constraints, optional parameters with defaults, and 4xx/5xx errors, all taken by reference from the owning modules. [US-004-02; BR-012]\n   - Example payloads for search and tagging. [US-004-05; NFR-009]\n   - The data-handling note on EU residency and on where active and archived note data is stored and served. [US-004-06; BR-013]\n   - The Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded. [US-005-01]\n   - Statements that the pinned field, the pinned-first ordering and the login endpoints are interface changes. [NFR-014 to NFR-016]\n3. **The endpoint-coverage metric** (`API-APIDOCS-827943`). This covers the share of implemented endpoints present in the document and the share of search and tagging endpoints carrying examples. [US-004-04; NFR-008, NFR-009]\n4. **The build-time checks on the document.**\n   - Spec parses and is valid.\n   - Documented field types match the code.\n   - The Scope section is present.\n   - Coverage is computed.\n   [US-004-02 AC3, US-004-04, US-005-01 AC3]\n\n**Dependencies.** The project spec's module map has `api-docs` reading every other module's operation records, and `scope-guardrail` reading the document. [backend.module-map]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Defining any endpoint, schema, status code or error code. The owning module specs and `backend.error-model` define them, and this module's document references them (core rule 6).\n- Minting `API-` or `SCHEMA-` ids. DevAgent mints them.\n- Versioning or publishing the `contracts/` package. The orchestrator does that.\n- Rejecting out-of-scope calls, and the contract test suite that asserts excluded routes are absent. `scope-guardrail` owns both. [US-005-02 to US-005-05]\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests (US-005-06), a product-management process (`GAP-BE-001`).\n- The residency compliance report and region scanning. `residency-compliance` owns them. [US-001-04]\n- The content of login, notes and search errors. `backend.error-model` and the owning modules define it. This module only requires that the document shows it (US-004-03 is shared with `auth`, per `backend.coverage`).\n- Any user interface that renders the document.\n- Retention deletion. This module stores nothing that NFR-019 covers (`ASM-BE-506`).\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-506` onward, `GAP-BE-714` onward, `INV-BE-210` onward. It mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. Every other id is cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**This module owns no database tables, columns, indexes or migrations** (`ASM-BE-506`).\n\n- No input states any persistence for the document or the metric.\n- `backend.migration-plan` lists no `api-docs` migration.\n- NFR-019 names no `api-docs` record type.\n\nIt owns the following artefacts, none of which is a table.\n\n| Artefact | Kind | Produced from | Consumed by | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Published OpenAPI document | generated, shipped with the build (`ASM-BE-507`) | route registry and operation records of every module; `SCHEMA-` definitions of the owning modules; the Scope section; the data-handling note; examples | `API-APIDOCS-098363`; the coverage check; the `scope-guardrail` contract test [US-005-05] | Same content as `contracts/openapi.yaml`, different representation (`ASM-BE-513`). |\n| Scope section source | authored content, source-controlled (`ASM-BE-506`) | humans | the document build | Changes need review and approval before merge. [US-005-01 AC2] |\n| Data-handling note source | authored content, source-controlled | humans | the document build | Content fixed by FR-014 and FR-015. |\n| Example payloads | authored content kept with the document source | humans | the document build; the example-coverage count | Source module not stated (`ASM-BE-506`). |\n| Coverage result | computed, not stored | published document compared with the route registry of the same build | `API-APIDOCS-827943`; the CI check | Carries: implemented count, documented count, percentage, names of missing endpoints, search/tagging endpoint count, count with examples, percentage. History is not stored (`GAP-BE-720`). |\n\n**Referential integrity.** There are no foreign keys, so there is no `ON DELETE` behaviour.\n\n**PII and encryption.** The module stores no personal data. It makes no encryption claim (`GAP-BE-304`). Examples in the document must contain no credential and no real-looking PII (`INV-BE-214`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nNo entity of this module carries a status column. The only lifecycle is that of one build's document. It is a build-time lifecycle and no API call triggers a transition.\n\n### 3.1 Document, per build\n\n| State | Meaning | Terminal |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GENERATED` | assembled from operation records, schemas, Scope section, data-handling note and examples | no |\n| `INVALID` | a check failed and the build fails | **yes** |\n| `VALIDATED` | all checks passed | no |\n| `PUBLISHED` | served by `API-APIDOCS-098363` and measured by `API-APIDOCS-827943` | no |\n| `SUPERSEDED` | a later build's document replaced it | **yes** |\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `GENERATED` | build runs | none |\n| `GENERATED` | `INVALID` | the generated document cannot be parsed | none [US-004-04 AC3] |\n| `GENERATED` | `INVALID` | the Scope section is missing | none [US-005-01 AC3] |\n| `GENERATED` | `INVALID` | a documented field type differs from the code | mechanism `GAP-BE-718` [US-004-02 AC3] |\n| `GENERATED` | `INVALID` | at least one implemented endpoint is absent from the document | `ASM-BE-509` [BR-012; NFR-008] |\n| `GENERATED` | `VALIDATED` | none of the above applies | all four checks pass |\n| `VALIDATED` | `PUBLISHED` | deployment | the document ships in the same artefact as the routes (`ASM-BE-507`), so an endpoint is documented no later than it is callable [US-004-01 AC2] |\n| `PUBLISHED` | `SUPERSEDED` | a later build is `PUBLISHED` | none |\n\n**Illegal transitions.** There is no HTTP status or error code, because no operation changes state. A failed check is a failed build, not a response.\n\n**Example coverage.** Below 100% example coverage does not move a document to `INVALID` (`ASM-BE-509`).\n\n### 3.2 Runtime reads\n\n- `API-APIDOCS-098363` returns the `PUBLISHED` document of the running build.\n- `API-APIDOCS-827943` computes the metric from that document and the same build's route registry (`ASM-BE-507`). It does not mutate state.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-001 Serve the published document\n**Given** a deployed notes_api with a `PUBLISHED` document,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-098363` is called with an Accept header the service can satisfy,\n**Then** it returns 200 with a valid OpenAPI 3.x document in the JSON representation (`GAP-BE-716`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC1); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-serve-valid-openapi3` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-002 List every implemented endpoint\n**Given** the route registry of the build,\n**When** the document is generated,\n**Then** it contains an entry for every implemented endpoint, with no exception.\n- It includes CRUD, tagging, search and archive endpoints, the email one-time-code login endpoints `POST /auth/otp/send` and `POST /auth/otp/verify`, and both operations of this module.\n- It includes every other implemented endpoint of every module.\n- An endpoint is \"present\" when the document has an operation with the same method and path, after path-template normalisation (`ASM-BE-512`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC1); BR-012; NFR-008; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-lists-all-endpoints`, `test-apidocs-includes-login-endpoints` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Paths of several endpoints are `GAP-BE-303`; the document uses whatever paths the owning modules' operation records carry.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-003 No endpoint callable before documented\n**Given** a new endpoint added to notes_api,\n**When** the document is regenerated and published,\n**Then** the new endpoint is in the document no later than it becomes callable (`ASM-BE-507`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC2).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-new-endpoint-listed-in-same-build` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build step).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-004 Unsupported Accept header\n**Given** a request to `API-APIDOCS-098363` whose Accept header the service cannot satisfy,\n**When** the request is handled,\n**Then**, by default, it returns 200 with the JSON document and a content type that names JSON explicitly. It does not return 406 (`ASM-BE-510`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (AC3).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-accept-unsupported-fallback` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** The story allows 406 or a fallback; the choice is `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-005 Document schemas, constraints and errors by reference\n**Given** an endpoint that accepts a payload or can fail,\n**When** the document entry is generated,\n**Then** it states the request and response by reference to the owning module's `SCHEMA-` definition. It states required fields, types and constraints (such as title length and tag format) as the owner defines them. It states its 4xx and 5xx responses as `backend.error-model` and the owning module define them.\n- It restates no shape of its own (core rule 6).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC1); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-entry-has-schemas-and-errors` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Constraint values are `GAP-BE-035`. The error envelope is `GAP-BE-300`. Neither is invented here.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-006 Optional parameters and defaults\n**Given** an endpoint with optional query parameters (for example search filters),\n**When** the entry is reviewed,\n**Then** each optional parameter is marked optional and its default is documented.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC2).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-optional-params-have-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** The default and maximum page size are `GAP-BE-500`. Until they are decided, that default is documented as not stated.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-007 Schema drift fails the build\n**Given** a documented field type that differs from the type in code,\n**When** the build's spec-validation check runs,\n**Then** the check fails and names the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (AC3).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-type-drift-fails-ci` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build step).\n- **Status.** The comparison mechanism is `GAP-BE-718`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-008 Coverage at 100%\n**Given** every implemented endpoint is present in the document,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-827943` is called,\n**Then** it reports 100% endpoint coverage.\n- \"100%\" is reported only when documented count equals implemented count (`ASM-BE-511`, `INV-BE-211`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (AC1); NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-full` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Status.** Whether the endpoint requires authentication is `GAP-BE-715`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-009 Coverage below 100% names the gap\n**Given** one implemented endpoint is absent from the document,\n**When** the coverage check runs,\n**Then** it reports less than 100% and names each missing endpoint.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (AC2).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-names-missing`, `test-apidocs-coverage-no-rounding` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-010 Malformed spec gives no coverage figure\n**Given** the spec cannot be parsed,\n**When** the coverage check runs,\n**Then** it fails with a clear parsing error and reports no coverage figure.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (AC3).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-malformed-spec-parse-error` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Status.** The HTTP status and error code when the endpoint is called are `GAP-BE-717`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-011 Search examples\n**Given** the search endpoint is documented,\n**When** a developer views the document,\n**Then** it shows an example request with query parameters and an example response body. It also shows an example empty-result response.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05; NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-search-examples`, `test-apidocs-search-empty-example` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-012 Tagging examples\n**Given** the tag add and tag remove endpoints are documented,\n**When** a developer views the document,\n**Then** each carries an example request and response.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-tagging-examples` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Which operations count as \"tagging\" is `ASM-BE-508`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-013 Example coverage metric\n**Given** the document and its examples,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-827943` is called,\n**Then** it reports how many search and tagging endpoints exist and how many carry examples. The target is 100%.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-example-coverage-count` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-014 Data-handling note\n**Given** the published document,\n**When** an operations engineer reviews its data-handling note,\n**Then** the note states that all note data is stored and served within the EU.\n- It states that archived and active notes are held in the same store, because archiving is a flag and not a move (BR-007). No difference in storage location is called out.\n- It names no specific region, because the approved region list is not supplied (`Q-BE-151`).\n- It makes no encryption claim (`GAP-BE-304`).\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06 (AC1, AC2); BR-013; BR-001; BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-data-handling-note-present` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-015 Data-handling note: login email address\n**Given** the login operations transmit an email address through an email transport,\n**When** the data-handling note is written,\n**Then** the note says nothing about the region of that transmission until it is decided.\n- It must not claim that all personal data stays in the EU.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06; NFR-017 (corrupted as supplied).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-note-makes-no-unsupported-claim` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** **BLOCKED** on `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006` (Critical). Only this requirement is blocked; the rest of the module is buildable.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-016 Scope section\n**Given** the published document,\n**When** a reader views its Scope section,\n**Then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (AC1); BR-014 to BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-section-lists-exclusions` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-017 Scope changes are reviewed\n**Given** a proposed change that adds or alters an out-of-scope item,\n**When** the change is submitted,\n**Then** it requires review and approval before merge.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (AC2).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-change-needs-approval` (planned; merge-rule check).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The approver is `GAP-BE-719`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-018 Missing Scope section fails the build\n**Given** a build whose document has no Scope section,\n**When** the documentation build runs,\n**Then** it fails with a lint or validation error.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01 (AC3).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-lint-missing-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build step).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-019 Interface changes are stated\n**Given** the pinned field and pinned-first ordering, and the login send and verify endpoints,\n**When** the document is published,\n**Then** each is described as an interface change that consumers must adapt to.\n- The pinned wording is on the operations that carry the field.\n- The login wording is on the send and verify operations.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014; NFR-015; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-interface-change-statements` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** How consumers are notified is `GAP-BE-721`. CR labelling differs between inputs (`CON-BE-019`).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-020 Incomplete coverage refuses publication\n**Given** at least one implemented endpoint absent from the document,\n**When** the build reaches publication,\n**Then** the build fails and the document is not published (`ASM-BE-509`).\n- **Traces to.** BR-012; NFR-008; US-004-01 (AC2).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-incomplete-coverage-blocks-publish` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build step).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-APIDOCS-098363` | GET | `/openapi.json` | `api-docs` | 200 | 406 | Return the published OpenAPI document for notes_api. The document carries every endpoint, the data-handling note (EU residency, active and archived notes) and the Scope section listing exclusions (attachments, real-time, mobile-specific). The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303). An unsupported Accept header is 406 or a fallback (GAP-BE-309), and whether the document is public is undecided. |\n| `API-APIDOCS-827943` | GET | `/openapi/coverage` | `api-docs` | 200 | 406 | Return the endpoint-coverage metric for the published OpenAPI document: how many implemented endpoints are documented and how many search and tagging endpoints carry examples. The target is 100% for both. The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303). Whether it requires authentication is undecided. |\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-098363` \u2014 GET `/openapi.json`\n\nReturn the published OpenAPI document for notes_api. The document carries every endpoint, the data-handling note (EU residency, active and archived notes) and the Scope section listing exclusions (attachments, real-time, mobile-specific). The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303). An unsupported Accept header is 406 or a fallback (GAP-BE-309), and whether the document is public is undecided.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI specification version of the document.\n- `info` (object, required): API title, version and description. The description states that notes_api is scoped to notes, search and email one-time-code sign-in.\n- `paths` (object, required): All documented endpoints with parameters, request bodies, success responses and error responses (including 401 and 403 for authenticated endpoints). Search and tagging endpoints carry request and response examples.\n- `components` (object, required): Reusable schemas, including the error envelope and security schemes.\n- `x-data-handling` (object, required): Data-handling note: all note data, active and archived, is stored and served within the EU, and archive endpoints do not differ in storage location. The approved region list is open (Q-BE-151).\n- `x-scope` (object, required): Scope section: lists attachment/file-upload, real-time channels (WebSocket, SSE) and mobile-specific endpoints as out of scope, with the message returned when they are called.\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-827943` \u2014 GET `/openapi/coverage`\n\nReturn the endpoint-coverage metric for the published OpenAPI document: how many implemented endpoints are documented and how many search and tagging endpoints carry examples. The target is 100% for both. The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303). Whether it requires authentication is undecided.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `total_endpoints` (integer, required): Number of implemented endpoints in notes_api.\n- `documented_endpoints` (integer, required): Number of those endpoints present in the published OpenAPI document.\n- `coverage_percent` (number, required): Documented endpoints as a percentage of total endpoints. The target is 100.\n- `undocumented_endpoints` (array, required): Method and path of each implemented endpoint missing from the document. Empty when coverage is 100%.\n- `search_tagging_endpoints_total` (integer, required): Number of search and tagging endpoints.\n- `search_tagging_endpoints_with_examples` (integer, required): Number of search and tagging endpoints that carry request and response examples. The target is all of them.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-210`. [US-004-04; `ASM-BE-511`]\n- `INV-BE-212`. Every published document contains the Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded. [US-005-01]\n- `INV-BE-213`. No idempotency key exists or is needed.\n\n**Ordering with the contract suite.** The `scope-guardrail` contract test reads the output of this module's document build. The web-app surface must adapt to the pinned field and ordering and to the login operations `POST /auth/otp/send` and `POST /auth/otp/verify`. | FR-019 | statements placed in the descriptive text of the affected operations | a communications decision naming the channel |\n| `GAP-BE-722` | the rate-limit class of both operations | `authorization` | none asserted | a rate-limit policy |\n\n### Inherited open items (defined in the project spec, cited not re-minted)\n\n| Id | Effect on this module |\n|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-303` | paths of several endpoints; the two paths of this module are proposals |\n| `GAP-BE-309` | 406 or fallback; also affects FR-004 |\n| `GAP-BE-035` | constraint values for FR-005 |\n| `GAP-BE-500` | page size default for FR-006 |\n| `GAP-BE-300`, `ASM-BE-300` | error envelope for FR-005 |\n| `GAP-BE-304` | no encryption claim in FR-014 |\n| `Q-BE-151` | region list; FR-014 names no region |\n| `Q-BE-005`, `CON-BE-006` | block FR-015 |\n| `CON-BE-019` | CR label mismatch, affects FR-019 |\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| Id | Statement | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-506` | This module owns no tables. The Scope section, data-handling note and examples are source-controlled content. | No input states persistence; US-005-01 AC2 says \"before merge\"; `backend.migration-plan` has no `api-docs` migration. | a decision on `GAP-BE-720` |\n| `ASM-BE-507` | The document is generated from the route registry at build time and ships in the same artefact as the routes. The checks run on every build. | US-004-01 AC2 requires \"before or at the same time it becomes callable\". | the orchestrator or a platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-508` | \"Search and tagging endpoints\" means the operations of the `search` module and the tag add and tag remove operations of the `notes` module. | NFR-009 names \"search and tagging\" with no paths (`GAP-BE-303`). | path decisions |\n| `ASM-BE-509` | Endpoint coverage below 100% fails publication. Example coverage below 100% is reported and does not. | BR-012 has no exception and NFR-008 is Must Have. The status policy and registry list no 406 code. | `GAP-BE-309` |\n| `ASM-BE-511` | The coverage percentage is documented count over implemented count, and 100% is reported only on exact equality. | US-004-04 AC2 needs a visible shortfall. Path templates are compared after normalisation. Schema completeness is checked separately under FR-005 and FR-007. | US-004-04 defines coverage as presence in the spec. | confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-513` | The served document has the same content as `contracts/openapi.yaml`, differing only in representation. | Both derive from the same operation records and schemas. | the orchestrator |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06", "US-005-01"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: scope-guardrail\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: []\nscope: []\n```\nNo operation records were supplied for this module, so `scope` is empty. The module defines no endpoint of its own. It governs how every other route is rejected or absent, as the module map states. [BACKEND-SPEC.md backend.module-map]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `scope-guardrail` keeps notes_api inside its documented minimal scope. It owns four things. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-005-02 to US-005-05]\n\n1. **No attachment or file-upload endpoint exists.** A request to an attachment path, or a multipart file upload to a note endpoint, is rejected and persists nothing. [BR-014; US-005-02]\n2. **No real-time channel exists.** A WebSocket upgrade or SSE connection is rejected. Concurrent PATCH requests get a last-write or conflict response, with no live broadcast and no merge logic. [BR-015; US-005-03]\n3. **A clear, structured error is returned for out-of-scope calls.** The error carries a code such as `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a human-readable reason, so an integrator is not left with a bare 404. [BR-016; NFR-011; US-005-04]\n4. **A contract test suite proves the exclusions on every CI build.** It passes only when zero endpoints match the excluded categories (mobile, attachments, real-time). A new endpoint that matches an excluded pattern fails CI and blocks the merge. [NFR-010; US-005-05]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- The published Scope section of the API documentation, and the documentation build failing without it. Both belong to `api-docs`. [US-005-01]\n- The OpenAPI document that the contract test reads, which `api-docs` produces. [backend.module-map]\n- The global error envelope and the error-code registry, which `backend.error-model` owns. This module uses the envelope and `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and defines neither.\n- The outcome of a concurrent PATCH. The `notes` module owns the PATCH handler, and this module only requires that no broadcast or merge logic exists.\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests for mobile, attachments or real-time collaboration, which is a product-management process. [BR-017; `GAP-BE-001`]\n- Any sign-in, note, search or residency behaviour.\n\n**Operations owned.** None. The supplied operation list for this module is empty.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-556` onward, `GAP-BE-764` onward and `INV-BE-260` onward. It mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. All other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**This module owns no database table.** No input evidences a persisted entity for it. Rejected requests persist nothing. [US-005-02: \"persists no file content\"] So there are no columns, constraints, indexes or migrations here. The project spec's migration plan has no entry for this module either.\n\n**Exclusion list (configuration, not a table).** The module needs one logical structure, a list of excluded categories and the route patterns that belong to each (`ASM-BE-557`).\n\n| Field | Meaning | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| `category` | one of `mobile`, `attachment`, `real-time` | BR-016; US-005-05 |\n| `pattern` | a path pattern matched against whole path segments | examples only: `/notes/{id}/attachments` [US-005-02], `/attachments` and `/ws` [US-005-05] |\n\n- Where the list is stored (source code, a file or a database) is not stated (`GAP-BE-764`).\n- The patterns for `mobile` are not given in any input (`GAP-BE-764`).\n- The list is read by both the runtime rejection and the contract test. It is defined once and referenced by both (core rule 6).\n- No PII is held, no timestamps are kept, and nothing is encrypted or retained.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n**No entity has a status.** Each request is classified on its own, so there are no states, no terminal states and no illegal transitions. The behaviour is a decision table.\n\n| Request | Outcome | Persists | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Any method to an attachment path, e.g. `POST /notes/{id}/attachments` | rejected with status 404, `error.code` `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a reason message (`ASM-BE-556`, `CON-BE-007`) | nothing | US-005-02; US-005-04; BR-016 |\n| `multipart/form-data` with a file payload to a note create or update endpoint | rejected with status 415 and `error.code` `OUT_OF_SCOPE` (`ASM-BE-558`) | no file content | US-005-02; US-005-04 |\n| WebSocket upgrade, or SSE connection, to any endpoint | connection rejected, no protocol upgrade, a standard error status. The exact status and the SSE detection rule are `GAP-BE-765`. | nothing | US-005-03; BR-015 |\n| Call to a mobile-specific endpoint | clear, documented error per BR-016. Patterns, status and code are `GAP-BE-764`, `GAP-BE-765`. | nothing | BR-016 |\n| Two concurrent PATCH requests to one note | processed with a standard last-write or conflict response and no live broadcast or merge. Which of the two is `GAP-BE-766`. | per the `notes` module | US-005-03 |\n\n**Guards.**\n\n- Classification of a path request uses whole path segments of the exclusion list (`ASM-BE-557`).\n- The order against authentication and validation is `GAP-BE-767`.\n- Status 415 is shared with the resend limit of `POST /auth/otp/send`. Clients discriminate on `error.code`, as `backend.error-model` states. [BR-028]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-001 No attachment endpoint\n**Given** the deployed API route registry,\n**When** a request is sent to any attachment path (e.g. `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`),\n**Then** the API returns 404 with a structured out-of-scope error (`ASM-BE-556`), and no route for that path is registered.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02; BR-014; US-005-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-attachment-path-404` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The 404-versus-message tension is `CON-BE-007`. The attachment path patterns are `GAP-BE-764`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-002 Multipart file upload rejected\n**Given** a note create or update endpoint,\n**When** a `multipart/form-data` request with a file payload is submitted,\n**Then** the API returns 415 with `error.code` `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a human-readable reason, and persists no file content and no partial note.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02; US-005-04; BR-014.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-multipart-415`, `test-scope-multipart-persists-nothing` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** Whether a multipart request without a file is also rejected is `ASM-BE-558`, `GAP-BE-769`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-003 No WebSocket or SSE\n**Given** the running API server,\n**When** a client attempts a WebSocket upgrade or an SSE connection to any notes_api endpoint,\n**Then** the connection is rejected, no protocol upgrade occurs, and a standard error status is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03; BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-websocket-upgrade-rejected`, `test-scope-sse-rejected` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The status, the error code and the SSE detection rule are `GAP-BE-765`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-004 No broadcast or merge on concurrent PATCH\n**Given** two clients submit PATCH requests to the same note concurrently,\n**When** both requests are processed,\n**Then** the API applies a standard last-write or conflict response, with no live broadcast and no merge logic.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03; BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The choice between last-write and conflict is `GAP-BE-766`. The PATCH handler belongs to `notes`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-005 Structured out-of-scope error\n**Given** an integrator calls an out-of-scope endpoint, or sends a multipart file upload to a notes endpoint,\n**When** the request is rejected,\n**Then** the response body uses the global error envelope, includes a message referencing the scope exclusion, and carries a structured error code (`OUT_OF_SCOPE`) with a human-readable reason.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-04; BR-016; NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-error-body-shape` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** `OUT_OF_SCOPE` is cited for the upload case only. Using it for every category is `ASM-BE-559`. The envelope is `ASM-BE-300`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-006 Contract test over the published spec\n**Given** the CI pipeline runs,\n**When** the scope-guardrail test suite executes against the OpenAPI document,\n**Then** it passes only when zero endpoints match the excluded categories (mobile, attachments, real-time).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05; NFR-010; BR-014; BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-contract-spec-clean` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The document is produced by `api-docs` (`ASM-BE-560`). The patterns are `GAP-BE-764`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-007 Excluded pattern fails CI\n**Given** a developer adds an endpoint matching an excluded pattern (e.g. `/attachments` or `/ws`),\n**When** CI runs on the pull request,\n**Then** the guardrail test fails and the merge is blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05; NFR-010 (\"0 out-of-scope endpoints detected per build\").\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-contract-fails-on-excluded-route` (planned, with a deliberately excluded fixture route).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** Blocking a merge is a CI branch-protection setting. Which CI system is used is not stated in any input.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-008 Registry check\n**Given** the deployed route registry,\n**When** the contract suite runs,\n**Then** it asserts that no registered route matches an excluded category, independent of what the published document lists.\n- **Traces to.** BR-014; BR-015; US-005-02 (\"the deployed API route registry\").\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-registry-clean` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** How the suite enumerates the registry is `GAP-BE-768`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-009 Mobile-specific calls\n**Given** a call to a mobile-specific endpoint,\n**When** the request is received,\n**Then** it receives a clear, documented error rather than a silent failure.\n- **Traces to.** BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-mobile-endpoint-error` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `GAP-BE-764` (no mobile-specific pattern is given) and `GAP-BE-765` (status).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-260`. A request rejected as out of scope persists nothing: no file content, no note, no partial record. [US-005-02]\n- `INV-BE-261`. No route in the deployed registry or in the published OpenAPI document matches an excluded category. A build in which one does fails. [NFR-010; US-005-05; BR-014; BR-015]\n- `INV-BE-262`. No endpoint completes a WebSocket protocol upgrade or holds an SSE connection open. [US-005-03; BR-015]\n- `INV-BE-263`. Every out-of-scope rejection body carries a structured `error.code` and a human-readable reason. It never reveals a stack trace, a file name or any submitted content. [US-005-04; NFR-011; core 12]\n- `INV-BE-264`. Concurrent PATCH requests to one note are never merged and never broadcast to another client. [US-005-03]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Authorization\n\nThe module defines no operation, so no role-to-operation permission is granted or denied here. Following `ASM-BE-302`, nothing is granted that an input does not state.\n\n| Behaviour | Applies to |\n|---|---|\n| Rejection of out-of-scope requests | every caller, anonymous or authenticated. No input restricts the rejection by role. |\n| Running the contract suite in CI | pipeline and engineering actors. Neither is an API role in any input. [`GAP-BE-085`] |\n\n- **Ownership predicate.** None. The module touches no user-scoped resource.\n- **Ordering.** Whether authentication (401) runs before the scope rejection is `GAP-BE-767`.\n- **No disclosure.** A rejection must not reveal whether a note id exists. This follows from `INV-BE-263` and is not cited in an input.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work.** None. The module has no sweep, no retry and no queue.\n- **CI execution.** The contract suite runs on every CI build and on every pull request. [NFR-010; US-005-05] This is a pipeline step, not a service job. The CI system is not named in any input.\n- **Idempotency.** A rejection has no side effect (`INV-BE-260`), so repeating a rejected request gives the same response. No idempotency key is needed.\n- **Retention.** None, because nothing is stored.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Test plan\n\nNo test framework is named in any input (`GAP-BE-089`). Test names are proposals.\n\n**Unit**\n- `test-scope-pattern-matches-whole-segments` (`/attachments` matches, `/notes/attachmentsx` does not) (`ASM-BE-557`).\n- `test-scope-error-body-shape` for FR-005.\n- `test-scope-multipart-content-type-detection` (`ASM-BE-558`).\n\n**Integration** (against a running server)\n- `test-scope-attachment-path-404` (FR-001).\n- `test-scope-multipart-415`, `test-scope-multipart-persists-nothing` (FR-002, `INV-BE-260`).\n- `test-scope-websocket-upgrade-rejected`, `test-scope-sse-rejected` (FR-003, `INV-BE-262`).\n- `test-scope-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast` (FR-004, `INV-BE-264`).\n- `test-scope-mobile-endpoint-error` (FR-009), pending `GAP-BE-764`.\n\n**Contract** (in CI on every build, per NFR-010)\n- `test-scope-contract-spec-clean` (FR-006, `INV-BE-261`).\n- `test-scope-registry-clean` (FR-008).\n- `test-scope-contract-fails-on-excluded-route`, proving the suite fails when an excluded route is added (FR-007).\n- `test-scope-login-endpoints-not-excluded`: the login operations under `/auth/otp/` match no excluded pattern, so the guardrail does not reject the CR-129 endpoints.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 11. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n### Contradictions (cited, not minted here)\n\n- `CON-BE-007` (non-critical). US-005-02 requires 404 for attachment paths. US-005-04 and BR-016 require a message referencing the scope exclusion, not a bare 404. Reading used: 404 with a structured body (`ASM-BE-556`). Both literal texts are met, but BR-016's \"404 ambiguity\" wording is not clearly satisfied. The decision is the human's.\n\n### Gaps\n\n- `GAP-BE-764`. The excluded route patterns are not enumerated. Only the examples `/notes/{id}/attachments`, `/attachments` and `/ws` appear, and no mobile-specific pattern is given. Where the list is stored is also not stated. Degrades FR-001, FR-006, FR-007, FR-009 and the data-model section. Closing artefact: the exclusion list per category (`BLOCKED` for FR-009).\n- `GAP-BE-765`. The status and error code for a WebSocket or SSE rejection, and the rule for detecting an SSE request (path, `Accept` header or other), are not stated. US-005-03 says only \"standard error status\". The status for mobile-specific rejections is also not stated. Degrades FR-003 and FR-009. Closing artefact: status and detection decisions.\n- `GAP-BE-766`. Whether a concurrent PATCH gets a last-write or a conflict response is not decided, and no version or ETag mechanism is named. Degrades FR-004. Closing artefact: a concurrency decision, which the `notes` module also needs.\n- `GAP-BE-767`. The order of evaluation of scope rejection, authentication (401) and validation (400) is not stated. Degrades FR-001 to FR-003 and the authorization section. Closing artefact: an ordering decision.\n- `GAP-BE-768`. How the contract suite obtains the deployed route registry is not stated. Degrades FR-008. Closing artefact: a test-harness decision.\n- `GAP-BE-769`. Whether a multipart request without a file is rejected is not stated. US-005-02 says \"with a file payload\". Closing artefact: a rule from the product owner (reading used: `ASM-BE-558`).\n\nExisting gaps referenced: `GAP-BE-001`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-309`, `GAP-BE-300`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- `ASM-BE-556`. An attachment-path request returns status 404 with a structured `OUT_OF_SCOPE` body. Reason: US-005-02 states the status and US-005-04 states a body referencing the exclusion. Confirmed by a decision on `CON-BE-007`.\n- `ASM-BE-557`. The exclusion list is configuration matched on whole path segments, not a table. Reason: no input evidences stored data for this module, and substring matching would reject legitimate paths. Confirmed by an answer to `GAP-BE-764`.\n- `ASM-BE-558`. Any `multipart/form-data` request to a notes endpoint is rejected with 415, whether or not a file is present. Reason: no input defines a multipart endpoint. Confirmed by an answer to `GAP-BE-769`.\n- `ASM-BE-559`. `OUT_OF_SCOPE` is the error code for every excluded category. Reason: it is cited for the upload case and no other code is named. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-300`'s closing artefact.\n- `ASM-BE-560`. The contract suite reads the document that `api-docs` publishes, and the document is available at CI time. Reason: US-005-05 tests \"against the OpenAPI spec\". Confirmed by the `api-docs` module spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-005-02", "US-005-03", "US-005-04", "US-005-05"]}, {"id": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "type": "openapi", "path": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "content": "openapi: 3.1.0\ninfo:\n  title: Backend API\n  version: 1.0.0\n  description: Operations the backend module specifications define, rendered from\n    their structured operation records.
